## Analyzer Architecture Summary

NeMo-Guardrails is a Kubernetes component. Dockerfile EXPOSE ports\: 1337, 5000, 8000. connecting to external services\: aiohttp, httpx, openai, redis, sqlalchemy, urllib. runtime dependencies\: Redis. 137 Python package dependencies (137 required), categories\: 1 grpc, 1 image-processing, 1 llm-sdk, 1 tokenizer, 1 web-server, 14 observability, 2 ml-framework, 2 numerical, 2 serialization, 3 web-framework. Python k8s client with 59 API call(s).
